# Agents · D · part 24

924 entries, DomainScaffolder to drift-auditor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **DomainScaffolder**: Scaffolds new domain scanner demo-app and workshop repositories with full parity to existing Accessibility, Code Quality, and FinOps domains. Generates repo structures, bootstrap scripts, CI/CD pipelines, Power BI PBIP, and screenshot automation.
